## Data

- [`data/items.json`](data/items.json) and [`data/items.csv`](data/items.csv): every item with all fields, for use in tools, sites and mods.
- Item IDs are the game's own `ItemId` values, the same ones used in save files and in memory.

## Notes

- Built from the game data of Enshrouded 1.0. 769 unnamed, placeholder or unfinished internal entries are left out.
- Weapon, armor and most other item icons are rendered by the game from 3D models at runtime, so they have no image file. Their pictures here are the game's own item preview as shown in the tooltip; items without one yet show their category picture. More are added as they are captured.

## About this repository

This repository is purely an index. It contains no game files, code, mods or tools: only a browsable list of Enshrouded's items with their in-game names, texts and stats, for reference.

## Credits

All item icons and item pictures, item names, descriptions and lore come from Enshrouded and are the work of **Keen Games**. All credit for them goes to Keen Games. Enshrouded and all game content are © Keen Games; this index is not affiliated with or endorsed by Keen Games.

The category pictures used for items without their own icon are generic placeholders made for this index.
